- 1532 files · ~2,233,146 words

## Summary
- 32550 nodes · 66960 edges · 1324 communities (1133 shown, 191 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 90% EXTRACTED · 10% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 6617 edges (avg confidence: 0.93)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `12565bbd`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
